	-- Transaction
		Set @SQL = 'CREATE FUNCTION [YOMSArchitecture].[TransactionCode] (@YOMSConfigurationName varchar(200)) RETURNS varchar(max)' + char(13) + 'AS' + char(13) + '	BEGIN' + char(13) + '		-- Get Value Of Configuration' + char(13) + '		Declare @YOMSConfigurationValue varchar(15);' + char(13) + '		SET @YOMSConfigurationValue = (Select concat([ConfigurationValue] , cast(year(getdate()) as varchar(4)),''-'') From [YOMSConfiguration].[Configuration] Where [ConfigurationName] = @YOMSConfigurationName);' + char(13) + '		-- PRINT(@YOMSConfigurationValue);	' + char(13) + '	-- GET Max Count' + char(13) + '		Declare @YOMSMAXNO INT;' + char(13) + '		IF(@YOMSConfigurationName=''BOOKINGNO'')' + char(13) + '		Begin' + char(13) + '			SET @YOMSMAXNO = (Select ISNULL(cast(max(replace([OrderNo],@YOMSConfigurationValue,'''')) as int),0) + 1 From [YOMSOrder].[OrderHead] Where [IsPurchase] = ''false'');' + char(13) + '		End' + char(13) + '		IF(@YOMSConfigurationName=''PURCHASENO'')' + char(13) + '		Begin' + char(13) + '			SET @YOMSMAXNO = (Select ISNULL(cast(max(replace([OrderNo],@YOMSConfigurationValue,'''')) as int),0) + 1 From [YOMSOrder].[OrderHead] Where [IsPurchase] = ''true'');' + char(13) + '		End' + char(13) + '		-- PRINT(@YOMSMAXNO)' + char(13) + '	--- Zero Card' + char(13) + '		Declare @YOMSZeroCard varchar(50); Set @YOMSZeroCard = '''';' + char(13) + '		Declare @YOMSCounter smallint;' + char(13) + '		SET @YOMSCounter = (7-len(cast(@YOMSMAXNO as varchar(50))));' + char(13) + '		-- PRINT(@YOMSCounter);' + char(13) + '		WHILE ( @YOMSCounter > 0)' + char(13) + '		BEGIN' + char(13) + '			SET @YOMSZeroCard = concat(@YOMSZeroCard,''0'');' + char(13) + '			SET @YOMSCounter = @YOMSCounter - 1;' + char(13) + '		END' + char(13) + '		-- PRINT(@YOMSZeroCard)' + char(13) + '		-- Return Value' + char(13) + '		RETURN(CONCAT(@YOMSConfigurationValue,@YOMSZeroCard,cast(@YOMSMAXNO as varchar(50))));' + char(13) + '	END' ; Exec(@SQL);Print(@SQL);
